Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The translation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 665. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 567 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the translation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 665 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 567. QED.
